Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The spectral operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 683. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 7 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_683(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 683. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^7(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 211.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Every optimality condition deformation of the spectral object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 143. The moduli space has dimension at most 460 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 143 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the spectral structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 460. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 211.5 (Spectral Property)
